### ✓ 2026-08-07: the attention apex draws downward-causation conclusions from material that does not license them — three cited studies whose own direction runs the other way, plus an exclusion objection answered with an energy reply
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/apex/attention-as-causal-bridge.md
- **Notes**: **From convergent outer reviews (2026-08-07): clusters 4 and 5 in the synthesis, merged into ONE task because they share a single lens — *the article moves from a correlation, a dissociation or an energy fact to a downward-causation conclusion the cited material does not reach*. Do not re-split** (outer-review-same-file-task-pileup — this apex carries six open tasks; the other five own the gamma re-attribution, the Sjöberg stance, the L58/L146 COGITATE contradiction, the frontal/posterior localisation question, and the active-inference debt. **None of them owns anything below.**)
  ⚠️ **MINTED AT P2, NOT P1, DELIBERATELY — and the reason is the whole lesson of this cycle.** Part A is convergent (2 of 3 reviewers on each item) but **UNCHECKED at the primary sources**: nobody read Naccache et al. 2005 or Desmurget et al. 2009 this cycle. On 2026-08-07 two of three reviewers were verified **wrong** on their headline charges, so agreement on an unread paper is a reason to look, not evidence. **VERIFY EACH ITEM AT THE PUBLISHER BEFORE EDITING, and if an item does not check out, say so and leave the text alone** (replenish-asserted-concrete-defect-often-false, citation-verify-false-negative).
  **PART A — WHAT THE CITED STUDIES ACTUALLY MEASURED. Four items, ranked.**
  **(A1) Naccache et al. 2005 — CONVERGENT (chatgpt + claude), the highest-value item, UNCHECKED.** ChatGPT calls it *"the gravest citation-fidelity problem in the article"*: *"The source explicitly develops the epiphenomenalist interpretation the apex claims the evidence undermines"* — i.e. the authors themselves suggest conscious effort may be **a by-product of executive attention that does not causally regulate its deployment**. Claude reaches the compatible verdict from a different angle: the paper is Dehaene-lab global-workspace work, and *"the Naccache autonomic residue is offered as though it settles this, but it does not discriminate the two readings."* **The article-side fact is already verified**: **L86** ends *"felt effort is wired into the regulatory chain and so does causal work, even if it does not transparently report on its own operation"* — a factive causal assertion. **⚠️ THE ARTICLE IS BETTER CALIBRATED THAN CHATGPT ALLOWS — do not over-correct.** The same paragraph already says *"These results **constrain** rather than **establish** the dualist reading"*, and L148 already concedes the dissociations *"cut against the simplest dualist identity too"*. **The defect is the single clause "and so does causal work", not the paragraph.** `tenets/tenets.md` **L95** (`^tenet-3-standing`) governs this exact phrasing: articles asserting consciousness does *"real work"* or *"genuine causal work"* **inherit that debt rather than discharge it, and should read no more confidently than that paragraph does**. So the minimum fix is executable in house even if the Naccache check is inconclusive: downgrade the clause to the standing the tenet allows. If the check confirms the authors' epiphenomenalist reading, **state it and answer it** — do not delete the datum (citation-framing-accuracy-lens: re-frame, don't delete).
  **(A2) Desmurget et al. 2009 — CONVERGENT (chatgpt + gemini), UNCHECKED, and the article uses it TWICE.** **L96** and **L174** both conclude *"consciousness contributing at the policy level"* / *"with consciousness contributing at the policy level"*, unhedged. ChatGPT: *"The intervention is neural stimulation → conscious intention. It demonstrates dissociability of intention and execution, not nonphysical consciousness determining policy. **A physicalist should actively cite this experiment.**"* Gemini reaches the same place: *"The dissociation is a structural feature of cortical topology, not an ontological demarcation between mind and matter."* **This one is largely assessable in house** — the direction of the intervention is not in dispute; what needs deciding is whether the apex's two sentences claim more than dissociability. **CURRENCY CANDIDATE FROM THE CLAUDE LEG, UNVERIFIED**: Karnath et al. 2010 reportedly argues direct electrical stimulation may **deactivate** rather than activate cortex. Verify at the publisher before citing; do not paste the reviewer's reference.
  **(A3) Cai et al. 2024 — CHATGPT SINGLETON, UNCHECKED, but supported by the article's own reference title.** **L96** says *"dopamine-deficient mice retain motor capacity but lose goal-directed initiative — they 'can move' but 'won't move.' Dopamine modulates the **decision threshold**, not motor capacity itself."* ChatGPT calls this *"materially wrong"*: the RIM-knockout manipulation impaired **fast dopamine dynamics** while baseline dopamine persisted, whereas actual depletion (reserpine) or receptor blockade **did** disrupt movement initiation — so *"dopamine-deficient"* mislabels the manipulation and the second sentence is stronger than the paper supports. **The article's own reference entry at L182 reads "Dopamine dynamics are dispensable for movement but promote reward responses"** — *dynamics*, not levels — which is consistent with ChatGPT's reading. **⚠️ SCOPE FENCE: THE INFERENCE IS FINE; ONLY THE DESCRIPTION IS IN QUESTION.** L96's disclaimer (*"consistent with rather than licensed by Cai et al."*) was independently praised by the Claude leg as *"a model of calibration"* and is what refuted Gemini's "god of the gaps" charge. **Do not touch it.** Fix the empirical description if the paper confirms the problem; leave the hedge exactly as written (condense-regresses-calibration-qualifiers).
  **(A4) The ~300 ms / ~280 ms "convergence" compares incommensurable anchors — CHATGPT SINGLETON, VERIFIED IN HOUSE, and it is a CLUSTER defect, not an apex one.** ChatGPT: Thura & Cisek's ~280 ms is measured **before movement onset** in **monkeys choosing reaches**; the Map's ~300 ms is a **post-cue** attentional deployment latency in humans. Different species, task, zero-point and dependent measure. **The Map's own text makes the mismatch visible**: `obsidian/concepts/attention-as-interface.md` **L159** — *"~280ms **before movement onset**, the selected target peaks… matching the ~300ms deployment time of willed attention. If these were independent systems, why share the same timeline?"* **Loci to sweep** (fix-by-file-leaves-string-siblings-live, defect-sweeps-must-include-archive-tree — grep `280` across all three trees): `obsidian/apex/attention-as-causal-bridge.md` **L72** (*"The 300ms timescale is shared with motor selection… This convergence is not coincidental"*) · `obsidian/concepts/attention-as-interface.md` **L159** · `obsidian/topics/structure-of-attention.md` **L111** · `obsidian/topics/attention-and-the-consciousness-interface.md` **L63** and **L149** · plus `hugo/content/` mirrors. **⚠️ L63 IS ALREADY PARTLY CALIBRATED** (*"compatible with both shared-substrate readings and with independent systems"*) — that hedge is about interpretation and does **not** cover the anchor mismatch, so add the anchor point without removing the hedge. **DO NOT DELETE THE CONVERGENCE CLAIM** — the honest fix is to state both anchors and say what would be needed to make them comparable, which is ChatGPT's own prescription: *"Do not call them convergent unless a common task, temporal zero-point and measurement criterion can be established."*
  **PART B — THE EXCLUSION OBJECTION IS ANSWERED WITH AN ENERGY REPLY. CONVERGENT (chatgpt + claude), VERIFIED IN HOUSE, AND THE CHEAPEST ITEM ON THIS LIST.** **L172** is the apex's only reply in this vicinity: *"Conservation laws are preserved because the energy for any selected action comes from ordinary metabolic processes."* Grep confirms the apex contains **no** occurrence of *overdetermination* and **no link** to `concepts/causal-exclusion-argument.md` or `concepts/causal-closure.md`, both of which exist in the corpus. ### ✓ 2026-08-07: the gamma-band figures are REAL and correctly-directed but misattributed to Koch & Tsuchiya — re-source to Wyart & Tallon-Baudry 2008 and scope them; do NOT delete
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/apex/attention-as-causal-bridge.md
- **Notes**: **From convergent outer reviews (2026-08-07, 2/3 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude]. Cluster 3 in the synthesis — DEFECT VERIFIED, CLAUDE'S DIAGNOSIS AND REMEDY REFUTED. Priority NOT raised by the synthesis pass: already P1, and the P1 was set on the correct diagnosis.** **THE TWO DIAGNOSES CONFLICT AND ONLY ONE IS RIGHT — READ THIS BEFORE EDITING.** The Claude review calls the "~55-65 Hz / ~75-90 Hz" gamma claim a **"blocking fabrication"** that "corresponds to no primary source" and "inverts what the actual gamma literature reports", and prescribes **deletion**. **BOTH CHARGES ARE REFUTED — verified this cycle at the primary source.** The numbers come from a real experiment: **Wyart, V. & Tallon-Baudry, C. (2008), "Neural dissociation between visual awareness and spatial attention," *J. Neurosci.* 28(10):2667–2679, DOI 10.1523/jneurosci.4748-07.2008, PMID 18322110**, whose abstract reports "distinct and independent neural correlates of visual awareness and spatial attention at different frequencies in the gamma range (30-150 Hz)" — awareness at **mid**-frequency gamma (54–64 Hz), spatial attention at **high**-frequency gamma (76–90 Hz). That is the **same direction** the Map states, so there is no inversion. The sibling ChatGPT review found this source independently and has the correct diagnosis. **DELETING A REAL, CORRECTLY-DIRECTED FINDING WOULD BE A REGRESSION** (citation-verify-false-negative — a reviewer calling a real paper fabricated is a known failure mode).
  **THE ACTUAL DEFECT IS THREE-PART: misattribution + an uncited locus + overgeneralisation.** (a) **Misattribution** — `obsidian/topics/structure-of-attention.md` L165–166 credits both bands to "Koch & Tsuchiya 2007", who do not report these numbers; the same false credit sits in `archive/concepts/voluntary-attention-control-mechanisms.md` L110–111. (b) **Uncited** — `obsidian/apex/attention-as-causal-bridge.md` L60 and `obsidian/concepts/attention-as-interface.md` L115 assert the bands with no citation at all. (c) **Overgeneralisation** — Wyart & Tallon-Baudry is one MEG paradigm (faint near-threshold visual stimuli, contralateral visual cortex, 240–500 ms post-stimulus); the Map presents it as fixed "operating frequencies" of consciousness and attention. Re-scope to the paradigm, not to a general law.
  **FULL LOCUS LIST — SWEEP ALL THREE TREES (defect-sweeps-must-include-archive-tree; `archive/` holds full serving bodies at live URLs):** `obsidian/apex/attention-as-causal-bridge.md` L60 · `obsidian/topics/structure-of-attention.md` L165–166 (table "Source" column) · `obsidian/concepts/attention-as-interface.md` L115 · `obsidian/research/attention-as-interface-hypothesis-2026-01-16.md` L160–161 (attributes to vague "MEG studies" — fix the note too, research-note-self-flagged-gaps-propagate-to-the-article) · `archive/concepts/voluntary-attention-control-mechanisms.md` L110–111 · plus the `hugo/content/` mirrors of each.   **ADD the Wyart & Tallon-Baudry entry to the References list of every article that carries the bands**, and keep Koch & Tsuchiya where it legitimately supports the four-way dissociation (apex L56, attention-as-interface L109) — that use is sound and must not be collaterally stripped.
  **WHILE HERE — check the adjacent table row.** `structure-of-attention.md` L167 credits "Willed decision | Theta (4-8 Hz) | Rajan et al. 2019" from the same uncited-table family; verify it at the publisher rather than assuming it shares the defect (replenish-asserted-concrete-defect-often-false).
  **ADJACENT LOCUS ADDED BY THE 2026-08-07 SYNTHESIS PASS — SAME DEFECT CLASS, VERIFIED BY GREP, CHEAP.** The untriaged ChatGPT leg found that **Rajan et al. 2019 appears nowhere in the apex** — `grep -n 'Rajan\|Nadra\|Mangun' obsidian/apex/attention-as-causal-bridge.md` returns **zero hits** — yet **L70** asserts the three willed-mode markers (*"frontal theta oscillations, bidirectional frontoparietal coherence, and additional frontal recruitment"*) with **no citation at all**. That is the same uncited-quantitative-claim defect as the gamma sentence, in the same article, and it is the exact hole through which the gamma misattribution entered. `topics/attention-and-the-consciousness-interface.md` L63 already cites Rajan et al. 2019 for these markers, so the source is in-house — **verify it at the publisher before copying it across** (ai_citation_metadata_unreliable; and note this task's own process finding below is precisely about a citation copied from a sibling instead of verified). ChatGPT also nominates the 2023 Nadra–Mangun "willed attention" review as the second missing apex reference; that one is **unverified** and needs a publisher check before insertion.
  **PROCESS FINDING WORTH RECORDING IN THE CHANGELOG ENTRY:** the Koch & Tsuchiya misattribution was *introduced by an internal review*. `reviews/deep-review-2026-03-11-attention-as-interface.md` L30 records the uncited claim as "**Resolved** — Added inline citation", the citation having been copied from a sibling article rather than verified at source. **And the fix is not even present today** — `attention-as-interface.md` L115 is still uncited. ### ✓ 2026-08-06: the sentientism research note still recommends the disenhancement reply the article has now retired
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/research/sentientism-2026-08-01.md
- **Notes**: **SOURCE-OF-PROPAGATION CLEANUP, NOT A NEW FINDING.** The 2026-08-04 refine of `concepts/sentientism.md` fixed the article; the note that seeded the error is still live and still recommends the retired move. **L84** records the disenhancement objection in its **hedonic-only** framing (*"pure hedonism appears to permit genetically eliminating pain capacity rather than eliminating the causes of pain"*) and **L89** tells a future writer that phenomenal value pluralism *"is a natural reply and should be deployed"*. **That reply is now known to miss the proposal actually in the literature**: Shriver, A. (2009), *"Knocking Out Pain in Livestock: Can Technology Succeed Where Morality has Stalled?"*, **Neuroethics 2(3), 115-124** (details verified at OpenAlex 2026-08-04) targets only the **affective** dimension of pain — modelled on cingulotomy and morphine — leaving nociception, positive valence and every other value-bearing feature intact, so value pluralism counts *for* the intervention rather than against it. **FIX**: at L84 distinguish the wholesale-ablation version from Shriver's affective-only version and cite Shriver; at L89 replace *"should be deployed"* with the article's current position — value pluralism answers wholesale ablation only, and the affective-only version is engaged on the narrower ground that negative valence is how a creature's welfare shows up to it from the inside, offered as a direction rather than a settled reply. Do **not** re-open the article; it is already correct. Per research-note-self-flagged-gaps-propagate-to-the-article the note is the origin, so leaving it live invites the next writer to re-derive the same error. **Note this file is also swept by the Korsgaard-locator P3 above (L87, L172) — if both run, do them in one pass.** Sync afterwards so the `hugo/content/research/` mirror agrees.

- **Notes**: **TWO LINKS, BOTH VERIFIED LIVE-BREAKING, AND THE JUDGEMENT CALL IS THE POINT — read before editing.** The 2026-08-04 `/check-links` run reported 418 broken internal links, but **374 (89%) are local-only false positives**: the Hugo dev server does not apply Netlify's `_redirects`, and those targets all have live 301s in `hugo/static/_redirects` (spot-checked and confirmed). Of the 44 genuine production 404s, **42 are in the exempt `/workflow/` tree** (34 on `/workflow/todo/`, the rest in changelog archives) — prose references to planned-but-never-created articles, which project convention treats as exempt and which must **not** be "fixed". **That leaves exactly two, and they are the only broken links anywhere outside `workflow/`:** (1) `obsidian/reviews/pessimistic-2026-05-19.md` **L92** — `[[topics/many-worlds-and-indexicality]]`; (2) `obsidian/reviews/pessimistic-2026-05-22.md` **L100** — `[[topics/voids/haecceity-void]]` (also malformed: `voids` nested under `topics`). **Both targets confirmed never to have existed** — `find` across `obsidian/` and `archive/` returns nothing, and neither has a redirect rule. **⚠️ THESE ARE NOT STRIPPED BY SYNC.** I checked: both strings survive into `hugo/content/reviews/`, so unlike the memory-slug wikilinks elsewhere in `reviews/` (which sync strips with a warning), these are **served to readers as dead links**. **THE JUDGEMENT — DO NOT SILENTLY REPOINT.** These are historical review records: they document what a reviewer wrote on a given date. Repointing the wikilink to a live article (`concepts/many-worlds`, `topics/indexical-identity-quantum-measurement`, `concepts/haecceity` are the nearest candidates, all confirmed to exist) would make the record assert a reference the reviewer did not make. **Preferred repair is to DE-LINK rather than re-point** — convert the wikilink to plain text so the record preserves exactly what was said while no longer serving a 404, the same discipline the corpus already applies to unverifiable quotations (de-quote, do not delete). If you judge re-pointing better, add a visible editorial marker saying the link was supplied later. **SCOPE: these two lines only.** Do not sweep `workflow/` — the 42 hits there are exempt by convention and touching them would be churn. Note the total is stable against the recorded ~417 baseline, so nothing has regressed; `topics/`, `concepts/`, `apex/`, `voids/` and `positions/` are entirely clean. Run `uv run python scripts/sync.py` after so the published pages stop serving the 404s.

- **Notes**: From [pessimistic-2026-08-04-quantum-divine-action](/reviews/pessimistic-2026-08-04-quantum-divine-action/) — eight issues, three High, **all one root and one edit**: the article treats *divine* as interchangeable with *universal-scope* and *creaturely* with *selective*. **NO NEW RESEARCH NEEDED** — every fix is already on disk in the article itself or in `obsidian/research/quantum-divine-action-2026-07-14.md`. **LENGTH IS NOT A CONSTRAINT**: `analyze_length` measured **1639 words** against the concepts **2500 soft** threshold on 2026-08-04, so there is ~860 words of headroom; do not condense anything to pay for these additions.
  **(1) HIGH — the ledger sorts by scope, not agent.** The peculiar column (L65) is headed "they arise from an omni-determining divine agent", but objections **5, 6 and 7** are each derived in their own text from *universal scope* ("if God sets *every* outcome", "determining countless outcomes throughout the universe"). Tracy's **selective** divine action — which the article itself introduces at **L49** — escapes all three exactly as the Map does. So the closing claim at **L80** is true and inert. Retitle the column to name scope as its criterion, and add the cell the article's own **L53** sets up and never fills: what separates the Map from *Tracy-style selective QDA*. **The article already contains the sentence that resolves this** ("The Map's channel is Tracy-like in *scope* (selective) but Murphy-unlike in *agent*") — follow it through rather than importing anything.
  **(2) HIGH — cross-article contradiction, and it is an over-concession-gets-ratified-not-merely-missed.** **L29** ("no experiment can distinguish a divine selector from a creaturely one") and **L74** ("no experiment can adjudicate between them") are contradicted by `obsidian/topics/selection-only-mind-influence.md` **L149** ("the Map's empirical predictions have to be sought in conditional structure rather than in outcome frequencies") and **L127** ("the tests that bear on the strict reading are conditional residual-structure tests rather than generic Born-frequency tests"); that article's **L39** is explicit that Born preservation "constrains the long-run *marginal* ... leaving the mind-conditioned distributions unconstrained." Murphy acts at *every* event and Plantinga at *all* collapses whether or not a mind is present; the Map's channel acts only where minds are, so mind-conditioned tests discriminate **in principle** against both versions the article foregrounds. Make the concession **scope-relative** (sound against selective divine action, false against universal-scope) and link `[[selection-only-mind-influence]]` and `[[ensemble-level-epiphenomenalism]]` at the point of concession. ⚠️ **DO NOT OVERCORRECT** into claiming a live empirical win — the sibling article is careful that these are a research programme, not a result, and it explicitly leaves the epiphenomenalism charge "live rather than settled". The QDA page currently links neither sibling despite listing `selection-only-mind-influence` in its own `related_articles`.
  **(3) HIGH — one-directional source mining; the dropped material is in the research note.** For all three critical sources the note assigned material to *both* ledger columns and the article kept only the column the Map escapes. `research/quantum-divine-action-2026-07-14.md` **L133**: Saunders is "The strongest general objection catalogue against QDA — **mine it for the shared-liability items**" — yet the article cites Saunders once, at objection **7** (peculiar, the labelling dispute), the one Saunders item that does *not* apply to the Map. Same note **L139**: Qureshi-Hurst & Bennett "Supplies both an **INHERITED** objection (amplification/empirical pathway) and a **PECULIAR** one (theodicy)" — the article cites them only under objection **4** (theodicy); inherited objection **2** cites nobody. Attach Qureshi-Hurst & Bennett to objection 2 and add a Saunders row to the **inherited** column for his general critique of the programme.
  **(4) MEDIUM — the missing inherited objection, and the highest-value single addition.** Research note **L54**: "Tracy's 'God of the gaps' worry ... **is a pressure the Map's selective channel must also answer**." The article states the worry accurately at **L49** and confines it to Tracy's self-criticism. It attaches to *selectivity*, and the Map's channel is selective. Add it as inherited objection 4 — a "mind-of-the-gaps" row; it is the only non-physics-facing liability the ledger would carry, and the only row that records something the Map bears and universal-scope QDA escapes. Verified absent from the neighbourhood: `grep gaps` returns nothing in `concepts/selection-only-channel.md` or `concepts/objections-to-interactionism.md`.
  **(5) MEDIUM — unearned differentiator inside the *shared* column.** **L61** "The Map independently rejects Many-Worlds under Tenet 4, removing one horn" reports a move QDA makes identically: the article's own **L39** records that Russell's NIODA *requires* "a *collapse* interpretation ... on which the indeterminism is ontological rather than merely epistemic." Delete "removing one horn" or attribute the rejection to both parties.
  **(6) MEDIUM — no Russell-authored work in the References.** NIODA is the flagship subject and Russell is quoted at **L37**, but refs 1–9 contain only an edited volume he first-names (ref 3). The research note's "Cosmology: From Alpha to Omega" entry supplies the fix: *Russell, Robert John. Cosmology: From Alpha to Omega: The Creative Mutual Interaction of Theology and Science*, Fortress Press, 2008 — the note's "Book-length statement of NIODA." ⚠️ **PROVENANCE, NOT FABRICATION**: the "nonphysical hidden variable" phrase **verifies verbatim**, but against the St Andrews Encyclopaedia entry the note explicitly marks "used for metadata cross-checking, **not as a primary citation**". Either verify in Russell's own text or attribute to the encyclopedia and add it to the references.
  **(7) MEDIUM — L78 refuses comparative wins, L80 banks one.** L78's symmetric Tenet 5 application (the Map "cannot claim that minimal creaturely selection ... is *simpler than*, and therefore truer than" an omni-determining agent) is **correct and must be preserved**; two sentences later L80 claims the decisive objections fall on QDA and not the Map, which is a comparative-burden win in the same currency. Items 1, 3, 4 and 5 each independently undercut it. Drop it, or qualify to what survives item 1 — that it buys the Map no advantage over its nearest rival.
  **(8) MEDIUM — decoherence is asymmetric and booked as symmetric.** Objection 2 **L62**: "The Map faces the identical problem at *neural* scales." A divine selector is not spatially committed and may act anywhere; the Map's must act in a warm wet brain, the regime where the objection is strongest. Shared in kind, asymmetric in magnitude, against the Map. Replace "identical" with the asymmetry.
  **LOW / same pass, cheap**: **L57** drop "load-bearing" (CLAUDE.md names it an overused intensifier) and move the "which no existing Map article assembles" aside out of reader-facing prose — the novelty claim itself **checks out**, so keep the substance; **L62** "Murphy's accumulation model is **widely judged** to fail" attributes a consensus to no one, cite Saunders 2002 or Qureshi-Hurst & Bennett 2021 (both already in the reference list); **L33** occasionalism plays three roles (contrast class, ancestor, QDA's failure mode at objection 5) in one hard-to-parse sentence — disambiguate; **ref 7** add `DOI: 10.1080/14746700.2022.2155914`.
  **⚠️ ONE QUOTE IS UNVERIFIED — DO NOT DELETE IT (citation-verify-false-negative).** **L43** Plantinga, "without in any way abrogating the laws of physics", appears in neither the research note's Plantinga entry nor its recorded quotations, and the note logs no full-text access. Verify against *Where the Conflict Really Lies* if a source is reachable; otherwise leave it and book the check. **ALREADY VERIFIED THIS SESSION, DO NOT RE-SPEND BUDGET**: Plantinga 2008 (Crossref `10.1080/14746700802396106`, *Theology and Science* 6(4):369–401) ✅; Qureshi-Hurst & Bennett (Crossref `10.1111/zygo.12663`, *Zygon* 56(1) 2021 — Crossref carries **no page field**, so "75–95" is unconfirmable there and is **not** a defect) ✅; Qureshi-Hurst 2023 (*Theology and Science* 21(1):106–121) ✅; the Murphy quote verifies **verbatim including "one or other"** — not "one or another", do not "correct" it ✅; all eleven wikilinks resolve, `[[selection-only-mind-influence]]` lives in `topics/` not `concepts/` ✅.
  **PRESERVE — these are the page's strengths and are not to be traded away**: the framework-boundary marking at L74–78 (the honest downgrade [direct-refutation-discipline](/project/direct-refutation-discipline/) asks for), the symmetric Tenet 5 application, the Murphy→Tracy→Map scope axis at L47–53, and the absence of any editor-vocabulary leakage. **ATTRIBUTION**: hold the file's existing `ai_system: claude-opus-4-8`, appending the executing model `+`-joined if it differs (dual-ai-system-format-is-plus-joined-string); bump `ai_modified` from a live `date -u`, strictly past (fork-future-dates-frontmatter-timestamps). **MIRROR**: run `uv run python scripts/sync.py` and confirm the changed strings in `hugo/content/concepts/quantum-divine-action.md` (obsidian-only-fix-leaves-defect-live-in-hugo).

### ✓ 2026-08-06: `interaction-problem-across-traditions` and `african-philosophy-of-consciousness` classify Tempels and Yoruba two incompatible ways

Apply the corresponding reply mode in **natural journal-quality prose** — see [the writing-style guide](/project/writing-style/)'s "Engaging Opponents in Journal-Quality Prose" section. **Do not expose mode labels in the article body.** The classification is editor-internal; it belongs in the refine-draft / deep-review changelog entry, not in the article. ### ✓ 2026-08-06: two live articles restate the choice-blindness detection rate as a per-participant figure when the published statistic is per-trial
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/source-attribution-divergence.md
- **Notes**: **UNIT ERROR, NOT A FABRICATION — DO NOT DELETE ANY CITATION.** Minted by the 2026-08-04 `deep-review` of `concepts/interpreter-module-narrative-construction-unity.md`, which fixed this same defect at that article's choice-blindness paragraph. Per fix-by-file-leaves-string-siblings-live the reviewed article's locus is a sample, not the population; the sweep across `obsidian/topics|concepts|voids|apex|positions` and `archive/` found **two more live loci**, both restating a per-manipulated-trial rate as a claim about how many *participants* noticed nothing.
  **THE VERIFIED FIGURE — already checked at the publisher on 2026-08-04, do not re-research.** Johansson, P., Hall, L., Sikström, S., Tärning, B., & Lind, A. (2006), "How something can be said about telling more than we can know: On choice blindness and introspection", *Consciousness and Cognition* 15(4), 673–692, restating their own 2005 *Science* study. Verbatim from the publisher PDF: *"Tallying across all the different conditions of the experiment, no more than 26% of all manipulation trials (M-trials) were exposed."* The unit is the **manipulated trial**. Each participant faced several, so a per-trial miss rate licenses no claim about the proportion of participants who noticed nothing across a session — and the error runs in the direction that *strengthens* the confabulation case, which is why it has survived repeated review (empirical-claim-fidelity-orthogonal-to-metadata-and-quotes). Note the five-author form above is the correct one; a four-author variant dropping **Lind, A.** was fixed in `obsidian/research/voids-narrative-void-2026-02-25.md` on 2026-08-04.
  **THE TWO LOCI** (line numbers will drift, match on the string).
  1. `obsidian/topics/source-attribution-divergence.md` **L81** — "the original study reports only that most participants failed to notice, and runs no individual-differences analysis." ⚠️ **Handle with care: this sentence's actual point is the *absence* of an individual-differences analysis, and that point is correct and must survive.** Repair the unit clause only — the study reports a per-trial detection rate of no more than 26% and runs no individual-differences analysis — rather than rewriting the sentence's function.
  2. `obsidian/topics/introspection-architecture-independence-scoring.md` **L87** — "Choice blindness: when a selected card was covertly substituted, most subjects accepted and justified the substitute." Compact summary line; restate as a per-trial rate. The same bullet's follow-on clause about 2020–2021 replication distinguishing concurrent from retrospective measures is fine and should be kept.

The repaired paragraph in `concepts/interpreter-module-narrative-construction-unity.md` is the other.
  **PRESERVE THE ARGUMENT.** Choice blindness remains a genuine confabulation exhibit at full strength; the datum both articles need is what participants do *after an undetected swap*, and that is untouched by the correction. Do not soften either article's conclusion, and do not add hedges beyond the unit fix (condense-regresses-calibration-qualifiers over-concession-gets-ratified-not-merely-missed). **SCOPE**: these two loci only. Do not expand either article, do not add a References apparatus where none is needed, and cite Johansson et al. 2006 at most once per file. **NOT DEFECTIVE, do not "fix"**: `topics/authorship-of-action-divergence.md` and `topics/phenomenal-authority-and-first-person-evidence.md` were both checked in this sweep and neither states a rate.

the Map has registered this alternative but ***not* adopted it**, because relocating the influence to context-selection alone would weaken outcome-selection to context-setting and thereby weaken this tenet."* L107 repeats it as a heading. **THE CLUSTER ADOPTS IT ANYWAY, UNDISCLAIMED.** Sharpest locus, driver-verified verbatim: `obsidian/concepts/coupling-modes.md` **L132**, sitting under a heading that literally reads **"The Map's Position"**: *"Consciousness primarily shapes the option space (basis) and sustains attention on relevant alternatives (timing), with outcome selection mostly following Born statistics."* That is path (c), stated as the Map's position, demoting outcome-selection to exactly the context-setting role the tenet says would weaken Tenet 3. **THE FULL LOCUS LIST (6 error loci / 9 files incl. mirrors; (4) `concepts/quantum-completeness.md` **L104/L106** — *"Physics determines Processes 2 and 3; consciousness participates in Process 1"* and *"Process 1 is **the only location**"*, which rules out the Map's own most-endorsed post-decoherence path; (5) `concepts/channel-class-taxonomy.md` **L90** — the exact inversion, claiming Tenet 3 *"finds its most natural expression"* in basis-choice, *"mind shapes the option space while physics determines outcomes within it."* **FAMILY U — THE ROOT CAUSE, FIX IT OR THE FAMILY REGROWS:** `concepts/coupling-modes.md` **L158** lets Tenet 2 *"decisively favour"* basis control, which `tenets.md` **L69** forbids — minimality there is an **empirical corridor, not a likelihood ranking**. Reading Tenet 2's "minimal" as truth-tracking is what licensed adopting the mechanism Tenet 3 declines. Second U locus: `topics/valence-and-conscious-selection.md` **L81**. **THE FIX TEXT ALREADY EXISTS IN-CORPUS — COPY, DO NOT COMPOSE.** `concepts/von-neumann-wigner-interpretation.md` **L94** is the model sentence: *"the Map acknowledges but does not adopt"*. `apex/born-preserving-causal-efficacy.md` **L167** supplies the reason: the relocation *"fails by thesis-drift"*. **THIS IS A RE-FRAMING, NOT A DELETION** (condense-regresses-calibration-qualifiers) — basis and timing control remain live *as registered alternatives the Map has not adopted*; what must go is their presentation as the Map's own position, and the demotion of outcome-selection to a Born-statistics residue. - **Type**: refine-draft

- **Notes**: Minted by the 2026-08-04 `refine-draft` pass on `concepts/episodic-memory.md`, which fixed the P1 convergent-review defect at that file's L81 ("Each retrieval makes the memory labile through reconsolidation"). That task's own note required re-grepping the defective string across `obsidian/`, `archive/` and `hugo/` before closing the family — a review's locus list is a sample, not the population (fix-by-file-leaves-string-siblings-live defect-sweeps-must-include-archive-tree). **The sweep was run. The population is eight live loci, not one, and only one has been fixed.** **⚠️ DRIVER ADDITION 2026-08-04 — THE SWEEP ITSELF MISSED ONE, IN THE TREE IT NAMED. Ninth locus: `archive/concepts/constructive-memory.md` L58**, plus its mirror `hugo/content/archive/concepts/constructive-memory.md` L62, reading *"**Reconsolidation**: Each retrieval makes the memory labile again."* — the defective universal essentially verbatim. Confirmed live-serving: `https://unfinishablemap.org/archive/concepts/constructive-memory/` returns **HTTP 200**, so this is a fully-served body, not a dead file (defect-sweeps-must-include-archive-tree). **Why the sweep missed it, which is the reusable lesson:** `episodic-memory` said "labile **through reconsolidation**" and this page says "labile **again**" — a grep built from the fixed file's wording cannot see the sibling's wording (narrow-grep-zero-is-not-proof-of-absence). When closing this family, grep on the *shortest invariant stem* (`retrieval makes the memory labile`, `every retrieval`, `each retrieval`) rather than on any one page's full sentence, and re-run across all three trees. ChatGPT's own review anticipated part of this: its site-level recommendation 4 says to "correct any remaining suggestion that every retrieval changes its trace" in **Personal Identity**, a file nobody minted a task for.
  **THE REFUTING SOURCE, already verified at publisher on 2026-08-04 — do not re-research from scratch.** Kida, S. (2020), "Function and mechanisms of memory destabilization and reconsolidation after retrieval", *Proceedings of the Japan Academy, Series B* 96(3), 95–106 (PMC7167366). Abstract, verbatim: "a retrieved memory is **not always** destabilized and that there are boundary conditions that determine the induction of destabilization and reconsolidation according to certain parameters, such as the duration of retrieval and the memory strength and age." Pair with Elsey, J. W. B., Van Ast, V. A., & Kindt, M. (2018), "Human memory reconsolidation: A guiding framework and critical review of the evidence", *Psychological Bulletin* 144(8), 797–848, DOI `10.1037/bul0000152` (Crossref + OpenAlex verified; note the year is **2018** and **Van Ast** is a co-author — a same-cycle reviewer got both wrong). Their abstract concedes considerable supporting evidence across procedures but reports "a number of inconsistent findings" and alternative explanations blocking a conclusive neurobiological inference, calling reconsolidation "a viable but hotly contested explanation." **Do NOT write that propranolol blockade failed to replicate, and do NOT write "replication crisis"** — that framing came from a reviewer who supplied no checkable source, it was not verified, and the Elsey abstract actually lists propranolol among the *supporting* procedures.
  **THE MAP ALREADY HAS THE CORRECT TREATMENT — this is a cluster-inheritance failure, not a research gap.** `obsidian/topics/reconsolidation-as-selection-window.md` **L45** already states it properly, citing Sinclair & Barense (2018) on prediction error: "the window does not open every time something is remembered; it opens when retrieval delivers a mismatch the existing trace did not anticipate." Propagate that sentence's calibration outward rather than inventing new phrasing. The fixed `concepts/episodic-memory.md` "Memory as Construction" section is the reference template for the conditional formulation.
  **THE EIGHT LOCI (all grepped verbatim on disk 2026-08-04; line numbers will drift, match on the string).**
  1. `obsidian/topics/reconsolidation-as-selection-window.md` **L75** — **sharpest instance: the file contradicts itself.** L75 reads "Because retrieval reopens traces... it is, with each act of remembering, lightly rewriting the record it remembers from", directly against its own L45. Fix this one first; it is the article the others should be able to defer to.
  2. `obsidian/topics/consciousness-and-memory.md` **L119** — "Every act of retrieval destabilises the memory trace." The bare universal, attached to Nader et al. 2000.
  3. `obsidian/topics/consciousness-and-memory.md` **L165** — "Memories are modified by every retrieval, contaminated by subsequent experience".
  4. `obsidian/topics/consciousness-and-memory.md` **L125** — "the self is perpetually rewriting its own history. The person you believe yourself to have been is shaped by every act of remembering." Downstream of L119; soften once L119 is conditional, do not delete.
  5. `obsidian/voids/inaccessible-past.md` **L72** — "through reconsolidation, each retrieval permanently alters the trace, with the 'original' progressively overwritten". **The strongest overclaim in the set**: universal *plus* "permanently", which outruns even the uncontested reading.
  6. `obsidian/topics/personal-identity.md` **L72** — "each retrieval alters the trace through reconsolidation". Reviewer-named. **This file carries a standing NEEDS-HUMAN length decision (~96 w over hard ceiling), so the edit here must be strictly length-neutral or shortening** (human-decision-task-mispicked-as-refine — do not let this become a condense).
  7. `obsidian/apex/phenomenal-output-causal-machinery-dissociation.md` **L123** — "reconsolidation reshapes the trace at each retrieval". Apex tier; also carries a vetoed condense task, so keep length-neutral.
  8. `obsidian/topics/memory-channel-interface-evidence.md` **L120** — "each recall destabilises the trace and requires reconsolidation under conscious direction". Note this one bundles the universal *with* the Map's interface claim, so the conditional rewrite has to keep the interface reading intact while narrowing the empirical premise.
  **PRESERVE THE ARGUMENT — this is a calibration fix, not a retraction** (condense-regresses-calibration-qualifiers over-concession-gets-ratified-not-merely-missed). Two claims get run together at every one of these loci and only one is defective. That retrieval is **reconstructive** — what reaches awareness is assembled, not played back — is well supported (Bartlett; Schacter & Addis; Loftus) and is what most of these passages actually need. That retrieval **destabilises the stored trace** is the narrower, boundary-conditioned claim Kida refutes as a universal. Separate them; keep the first at full strength; make the second conditional. The Minimal Quantum Interaction reading is *helped*, not hurt: a gated window names specific occasions on which the substrate is underdetermined instead of asserting permanent openness.
  **ARCHIVE AND RESEARCH TREES — flagged, decision deferred, do not silently rewrite.** The same universal is live in `archive/topics/memory-consolidation-and-transformation-of-experience.md` (two loci, including "Each conscious retrieval permanently alters the trace." and "Every recall rewrites the record."), `archive/voids/past-self-void.md` L62, and `archive/voids/memory-void.md` L57/L89. Archived pages serve full bodies at live URLs, so an obsidian-only count understates the reach — but they are also a preserved historical record behind an archive notice, and editing them rewrites that record. **Record a decision either way in the changelog rather than acting silently.** Lower priority still: `obsidian/research/voids-memory-void-2026-02-01.md` L173 and `obsidian/research/voids-past-self-void-2026-01-29.md` L158 are dated research snapshots that fed the defective articles (research-note-self-flagged-gaps-propagate-to-the-article).
  **SCOPE**: loci 1–8 only in one pass if length permits; if not, do 1, 2, 3, 5 (the outright universals) and re-mint for the remainder. Do not expand any of these articles, do not add a new section to any of them, and do not add the full three-citation apparatus to every file — cite Kida once per file at most and cross-link to [reconsolidation-as-selection-window](/topics/reconsolidation-as-selection-window/) for the boundary conditions.
  **ATTRIBUTION**: hold each file's existing `ai_system`, appending the executing model `+`-joined if it differs (dual-ai-system-format-is-plus-joined-string deep-review-fork-over-attributes-ai-system); bump `ai_modified` per file, `date -u`-checked and strictly past (fork-future-dates-frontmatter-timestamps). **MIRROR**: run `scripts/sync.py` and verify each changed string in the corresponding `hugo/content/...` file — a fork that edits only `obsidian/` leaves the defect live on the published page until the next pre-push sync (obsidian-only-fix-leaves-defect-live-in-hugo). **LENGTH**: measure each file with `analyze_length` before and after; every edit in this task should be length-neutral or shortening.

### ✓ 2026-08-05: `invertebrate-consciousness-as-interface-test` misses five 2024–2025 papers — three by authors the article already relies on
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/invertebrate-consciousness-as-interface-test.md
- **Notes**: From the literature-drift audit of 2026-08-04 (research area: `invertebrate`). **This is a missing-citation flag, NOT a staleness flag — say so if the fix note records a reason.** The median empirical citation year is **2021** against `ai_modified` 2026-07-08, a lag of exactly 5 years, which does **not** exceed the `median_year_lag_threshold` of 5. Check 1 is clean. `find_superlative_claims` returns **0**, so check 3 (empirical-record currency drift) is clean too. The signal is check 2 alone: five topically appropriate papers, none cited.
  **THE STRONGEST SIGNAL IS WHOSE PAPERS THESE ARE.** Three of the five are by authors this article already builds on, publishing later work on the exact claims it uses them for. This is not a general "field has moved" flag — it is the article's own sources having moved.
  1. **Gibbons et al. (2024)**, *iScience* 27(8): 110440, DOI `10.1016/j.isci.2024.110440` (open access). Nine authors, senior author **Lars Chittka** — whose *The Mind of a Bee* (2022) is the article's evidence base at **L61**. Bumblebees touched on one antenna with a 65 °C probe groomed the touched antenna more than the untouched one, and more than sham- or un-touched controls. The abstract opens by noting it "has been widely stated that insects do not show self-protective behavior toward noxiously-stimulated body parts, but this claim has never been empirically tested". Directly strengthens the insect half of the article's case, and does so with a *behavioural* marker rather than the cognitive-sophistication markers L61 currently lists.
  2. **Leung et al. (2025)**, *PLoS Biology* 23(7): e3003217, DOI `10.1371/journal.pbio.3003217` (open access). **Same first author and same senior pair (van Swinderen, Tsuchiya) as the article's reference 9, Leung et al. 2021**, used at **L91** for IIT-structure collapse under anaesthesia in *Drosophila*. The 2025 paper runs a massive library of univariate time-series features and finds wakefulness distinguishable from anaesthesia and sleep in flies across many of them; its abstract explicitly criticises reliance on features chosen without "principled, theoretical justifications" and warns of "researcher biases toward specific analyses". **Handle this as a calibration correction, not an addition.** L91 currently reads the 2021 result as IIT-specific support ("integrated information structure collapses"); the same group's later work suggests a broad family of measures tracks conscious level in flies, which weakens the inference that integrated-information structure *specifically* is what the anaesthetic disrupts. The article's "**Anaesthetic convergence**" constraint at **L119** survives — arguably it is strengthened, since the convergence is now measure-robust — but the IIT-specific reading should be hedged.
  3. **Brown & Birch (2025)**, *Phil. Trans. R. Soc. B* 380(1939): 20240309, DOI `10.1098/rstb.2024.0309` (**not** open access). Co-authored by **Jonathan Birch**, whose framework has a dedicated section at **L83–L89**. On when motivational trade-offs count as sentience evidence: the abstract grants that such evidence shifted opinion on crabs and insects, then presses three critic points — the trade-off/consciousness link is not fully known; *C. elegans* shows simple trade-offs via a mechanism "plausibly too simple to support conscious experience"; and pain can *interfere* with rational trade-offs. Their conclusion is that the nature of the trade-off and its neural substrate matter. **This runs against the article's stated preference.** L103–L107 adopt the universal coupling response as "most coherent"; a nematode clearing a behavioural bar with a substrate too simple to be a plausible coupling site is exactly the case that response has to absorb. Engage it in the "Natural Zombie Problem" section rather than filing it as support.
  4. **Elwood (2025)**, *Animals* 15(3): 445, DOI `10.3390/ani15030445` (open access, sole author). Elwood is the principal decapod-pain researcher. **This one fixes an existing gap, not just an absence**: **L79** asserts that decapods "demonstrate nociceptive responses, learned avoidance, and anxiety-like behaviour under threat" with **no citation at all** — the only uncited empirical claim in the article. Elwood's review is the natural source, and its own calibration matches the Map's: many findings are "consistent with the idea of pain", but "that does not mean that pain has been proved... we will probably never have conclusive proof", with reflexive responses explicitly partitioned off. Cite it *with* that hedging intact.
  5. **Barton & Barrett (2025)**, *Phil. Trans. R. Soc. B* 380(1929): 20240255, DOI `10.1098/rstb.2024.0255` (open access). "Embodied cognitive evolution and the limits of convergence." **This is a rival framing and must be presented as one.** They attack the "cognitivist gambit" — the assumption that cognitive processes involve representations independent of the sensory-motor specialisations tied to different body plans and ecological niches — and argue sensory-motor adaptations are intrinsic to cognitive evolution rather than confounds to be controlled away. That is a direct challenge to the article's central abstraction: reading three divergent architectures (**L113 scale independence**, **L115 architectural flexibility**, **L117 evolutionary independence**) as instantiating one substrate-neutral coupling question. It also bears on the convergent-evolution inference drawn from Feinberg & Mallatt 2016 at **L81**. The honest move is to name it as pressure on the abstraction, not to absorb it as another datum.
  **SEPARATE APPARATUS DEFECT, FIX WHILE HERE.** **L85** cites *Birch's* **Edge of Sentience* (2024)** in the body, but it is **absent from the References list** — the list runs to 10 entries and stops at Birch (2022) as entry 2. Add it.
  **DO NOT INFLATE CALIBRATION** ([evidential-status-discipline](/project/evidential-status-discipline/)). Five more citations do not move this article up the five-tier scale. Two of the five (Brown & Birch, Barton & Barrett) cut *against* the article's preferred readings and one (Leung 2025) narrows a claim it currently makes broadly. If the pass ends with the article more confident than it started, it has been done wrong.
  **VERIFICATION LIMITS — CARRY THEM, DO NOT LAUNDER THEM** (research-note-self-flagged-gaps-propagate-to-the-article). The session's WebSearch budget was exhausted (200/200), so this audit was run against the **OpenAlex API** instead — see webfetch-survives-websearch-exhaustion. Every one of the five is verified for **metadata** (authors, journal, volume, issue, pages, DOI, date) and for **publisher-supplied abstract text only**. **No full text was read for any of them.** Abstract phrasing may be quoted *as abstract*; add **no verbatim quotation** from the bodies of these papers and no claim about their internal methods beyond what is summarised above (quote-aggregator-ratification-corrupts-verbatim). Brown & Birch 2025 is paywalled — treat it as the most constrained of the five. Note also that the OpenAlex record renders Chittka's surname with a dotless "ı"; the correct spelling is **Chittka**.
  **LENGTH**: measured this run at **2,777 w, status `ok`** against topics 3,000 soft / 4,000 hard — only **223 w to the soft ceiling**, 1,223 w to hard. Five citations plus a hedge on L91 will cross the soft threshold if written expansively. Keep each integration to a sentence or two, prefer amending existing sentences over adding new paragraphs, and preserve the existing hedges and underdetermination markers (condense-regresses-calibration-qualifiers). Measure with `analyze_length`.

Two downstream loci contradict it for the SAME system (cryptochrome radical pairs) against the SAME baseline: `concepts/quantum-zeno-effect.md` **L61** ("Its coherence is microsecond-scale—roughly **three** orders of magnitude below the millisecond timescale of neural decision-making") and `concepts/quantum-biology-and-neural-mechanisms.md` **L74** ("its microsecond coherence sits about **three** orders of magnitude below neural firing timescales"). It was also being actively propagated: the now-closed Denton sweep task told future fixes to "COPY the already-calibrated sibling framing from concepts/quantum-biology-and-neural-mechanisms.md", quoting the three-orders wording as the model. **DO NOT touch** the generic "even microsecond coherence remains three orders of magnitude below millisecond neural firing" (qbnm L98 + 4 archive loci — arithmetically fine read as 1µs vs 1ms), nor the ~100µs-vs-~300ms comparisons in `timing-gap-problem` L62 and `quantum-neural-timing-constraints` L65 (different comparison, correct). **(2) HEDGE DROPPED IN TENET SECTION — `concepts/quantum-biology-and-neural-mechanisms.md` L168.** The Bidirectional Interaction paragraph reads "The epothilone B study **directly supports** this... **confirming** the microtubule-consciousness relationship runs in both directions", while the SAME article's body at **L124** concedes Khan et al. 2024 "also admits a classical reading (microtubules traffic GABA_A receptors, isoflurane's primary target)". Tenet paragraphs are the passages most often quoted in isolation (navigation-surfaces-carry-unreviewed-claims). **(3) LEAD RETAINS AN EXCLUSIVITY CLAIM THE BODY GAVE UP — same file, L56.** Lead says the binding problem "may require the physical holism that **only** quantum entanglement provides", but L144 now carries the Vicente et al. 2008 correction and concludes "The synchrony therefore does not evidence quantum binding", and the dedicated sibling `concepts/zero-lag-gamma-synchrony-and-the-quantum-binding-argument.md` concedes the phenomenon to the classical relay-hub account outright. `topics/evolutionary-case-for-quantum-neural-effects.md` L62 has the calibrated wording — entanglement is "**among** the candidate mechanisms that could supply" holism, with IIT offering "a non-entanglement-based holism". Soften "only ... provides" to match. Lead paragraphs are the truncation-resilient surface the style guide prioritises (denton-2024-first-biological-precedent-propagation — check the LEAD). ### ✓ 2026-08-04: `methodology-of-consciousness-research` L146 asserts the phenomenal-guidance premise flat — the one exposed locus the values-in-science research named and nobody fixed
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/methodology-of-consciousness-research.md
- **Notes**: **THE DEFECT IS THE PREMISE, NOT THE CONCLUSION.** Verified verbatim on disk this run at **L146**: *"Scientific practice depends on irreducibly phenomenal experiences — the [aesthetic sense](/concepts/epistemic-emotions/) guiding theory choice, the felt encounter with anomaly triggering paradigm revision, the phenomenal certainty distinguishing genuine confirmation from lucky prediction."* The **consequent**, two sentences later, *is* properly conditionalised (*"If these phenomenal dimensions of scientific work are causally efficacious, then…"*). The premise it rests on is asserted flat.
  **WHY THE LITERATURE THIS PASSAGE GESTURES AT INVERTS IT.** Ward (2021), *Studies in History and Philosophy of Science Part A* 85: 54–62, DOI 10.1016/j.shpsa.2020.09.006, distinguishes **four** relations a value can bear to a choice — it can *motivate*, *justify*, *cause*, or *be impacted by* — and concludes that the inductive-risk claim "makes most sense as a claim about values being needed to **justify** such choices". The Map needs **causation** under Bidirectional Interaction. Separately, Longino classes **aesthetic** preference alongside metaphysical and political preference as precisely the thing objectivity must be protected *from*, relocating epistemic authority to the community and making individual phenomenal response epistemically inert **by design**. So the values-in-science literature does not supply this premise; it withholds it. The research note's headline finding is that the apparent convergence is *"not convergent — the apparent agreement is an equivocation across Ward's four relations"*, and that [P-M2](/positions/methodology-and-calibration/#p-m2) does not even get to apply because its precondition fails.
  **THE FIX IS A HEDGE ON THE PREMISE. IT IS NOT A DEMOTION.** Do not weaken the Galilean-exclusion point, and do not touch the conclusion or the closing "expansion of scientific methodology" paragraph. Condition the premise the way this article's four already-guarded siblings condition theirs.
  **THE SETTLED TEMPLATE ALREADY EXISTS — INHERIT IT, DO NOT COMPOSE A NEW ONE.** `topics/duhem-quine-underdetermination-consciousness.md` **L94–L96** was rewritten on 2026-08-02 with exactly this apparatus and now carries Douglas's (2009) direct/indirect distinction, Rudner (1953), the contestation via Elliott (2011), and the new demarcation problem (Resnik & Elliott 2023). **This article already links `duhem-quine` four times** — point at it rather than restating the machinery, which is also the length-cheapest route.
  **PROTECT THE FOUR ALREADY-GUARDED LOCI — DO NOT SWEEP THE STRING.** Here fix-by-file-leaves-string-siblings-live applies in the **inverse** direction: the grep population is larger than the defect. ### ✓ 2026-08-04: `consciousness-and-the-major-evolutionary-transitions` L64 says the biological-individuality pluralism never asks whether a subject is a further kind of individual — Godfrey-Smith asks it, in the special issue the article already cites
- **Type**: refine-draft

  **WHY IT IS FALSE.** Peter Godfrey-Smith, "Individuality, subjectivity, and minimal cognition", *Biology & Philosophy* 31(6): 775–796, DOI 10.1007/s10539-016-9543-1, is devoted entirely to connecting kinds of biological individuality to the evolution of subjective experience. It is **the paper immediately following** Pradeu's "The many faces of biological individuality" at 31(6): 761–773 — i.e. it is in the *same special issue* that this article already cites at **L62** (body) and **L122** (reference 11). The article is refuted by the issue it invokes.

Godfrey-Smith's second paragraph, read this run from the author-hosted primary PDF and quoted in the research note at L75: *"Setting aside dualist and panpsychist views, it seems that the special features of subjects are organizational, and this organization will be the product of some sort of evolutionary process."* That is the Map's phenomenal-blindness thesis operating **one level down** from where the article currently states it — the bracket is visible in the text, and it has a cost. That is a better paragraph than the one on disk.
  **WHAT SURVIVES AND MUST BE KEPT.** The *criterion lists* really do stop short, and that half of L64 is defensible: the SEP survey entry `biology-individual` (Wilson & Barker) contains **zero** occurrences of "conscious", "phenomenal", "sentience", "experience", or "person", and the most recent general criterion on offer (Fulda 2023, *Evolution & Development* 25(6): 353–370) is **agential** rather than phenomenal. So "the enumeration stops" stands; "the framework does not ask" does not. Fix the second clause, keep the first.
  **SCOPE — ONE SENTENCE PLUS ONE REFERENCE LINE. ### ✓ 2026-08-04: empirical precision and a 2020s literature layer for `phenomenology-of-memory-and-the-self`
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/phenomenology-of-memory-and-the-self.md
- **Notes**: The bibliography's foundations are fine; its *empirical synthesis* is behind the field — 29 references, few clearly post-2020, used to make claims about a fast-moving literature. **Three verified over-statements to fix.** (a) **L96: "anti-correlated with accuracy in the conditions that matter"** (verbatim). Either name a specific paradigm, population and outcome measure, or weaken to the well-supported claim that felt rightness does not guarantee accuracy. Wardell, V., & Palombo, D. J. (2024), "Stability and malleability of emotional autobiographical memories", *Nature Reviews Psychology*, DOI 10.1038/s44159-024-00312-1 (verified) supports context-dependent variation. (b) **L84: "Every act of remembering is also reinterpretation"** — narrow to a conditional, per the reconsolidation boundary conditions in Kida 2020 (see the sibling `episodic-memory` task; same defect family, re-grep the string across all three trees). (c) **L90/L116: the phenomenal-vs-propositional binary.** The article says propositional content persists reliably while phenomenal content is fragmentary, and separately concedes at L116 that a "smooth-gradient finding" would weaken its reading — the reviewer's sharpest point is that the emerging literature already makes that gradient live, via episodic narrative personal semantics and multidimensional retrieval models. That makes this a live confidence question, not a decorative citation update. **Verified additions (all checked at Crossref/OpenAlex 2026-08-04):** Perrin, D., & Sant'Anna, A. (2022), "Episodic memory and the feeling of pastness: from intentionalism to metacognition", *Synthese* 200(2), DOI 10.1007/s11229-022-03567-4 — the sequel to the Perrin/Michaelian/Sant'Anna 2020 paper already cited at L100, and it argues directly against the Fernández intentionalism the article discusses at L102; Chiorri, C., & Vannucci, M. (2024), *Journal of Intelligence* 12(2), 21, DOI 10.3390/jintelligence12020021, on retrieval-*process* versus retrieved-*product* phenomenology; Su, H., et al. (2026), "Retention and transformation of internal experiences in autobiographical memory narratives", *Communications Psychology*, DOI 10.1038/s44271-026-00425-8; Palombo et al. (2015) for SDAM as a check on the universality of "double presence" and the immediate "that was me" at L62. **Markostamou, I., Tanguay, A. F. N., & Kvavilashvili, L. (2026), "Retrieval processes, phenomenology, and importance for self-identity of unique vs. repeated events in autobiographical memory", *Scientific Reports*, DOI 10.1038/s41598-026-63043-8** is real but was published 2026-08-03, one day before the review — **it is absent from OpenAlex and EuropePMC and confirmable only at Crossref**, so do not conclude it is fabricated if an aggregator lookup misses it. It bears on the article's focus on unique, scene-like episodes. **APPENDED 2026-08-04 from `reviews/outer-review-2026-08-04-gemini-2-5-pro.md` — this is the one genuinely new, verified finding that review produced, and it belongs here rather than in a ninth task on this file.** **De Brigard, F. (2024), "Episodic memory without autonoetic consciousness", *Phil. Trans. R. Soc. B* 379(1913), 20230410, DOI 10.1098/rstb.2023.0410 is absent from this article entirely** (grep: zero occurrences of "De Brigard"), and it is the single most direct published challenge to the article's central premise. Abstract retrieved at EuropePMC 2026-08-04, verbatim: it "argues that the construct of autonoetic consciousness lacks validity and that, even if it was valid, it would still not be necessary for episodic memory", proposing a functional/computational characterisation on which "its characteristic phenomenology is a **contingent** feature of the retrieval process". The article's argument runs through autonoesis being *constitutive* and irreducible — L60 ("no analogue in perception or imagination"), L116 (the noetic/autonoetic contrast), L148 ("the irreducibility of autonoetic experience" supports Dualism) — so a construct-validity attack is a sharper threat than the "smooth-gradient finding" L116 already names as a weakener, and it is distinct from it: De Brigard attacks whether the construct is *valid at all*, not whether it grades. **DO NOT RESEARCH THIS FROM SCRATCH — the Map has already done the work and this article failed to inherit it.** `concepts/anoetic-noetic-autonoetic-consciousness` L70 engages De Brigard 2024 substantively and accurately ("lacks construct validity and is not necessary for episodic memory... treating phenomenology as a contingent feature—present in typical cases but dispensable in principle"), `apex/phenomenal-output-causal-machinery-dissociation` L163 engages it again, and `research/autonoetic-consciousness-2026-02-24.md` L43-138 holds a full research treatment calling it "a major recent challenge". The citation has been publisher-verified by prior deep-reviews at least three times. So this is a **cluster-inheritance failure of exactly the shape already flagged in the sibling P2 convergence task** — the cautious sibling and the unqualified target are one click apart. Import the existing engagement and give it a real reply; if the reply is not decisive, restrict the claim at L148 rather than adding a fourth confession. **Correct the reviewer's framing if it is quoted anywhere:** Gemini wrote that "the text briefly cites De Brigard's deflationary view but fails to grapple with" it — **false, the article does not cite him at all**; the reviewer appears to have imported the sibling concept page's contents and attributed them to the target.

### ✓ 2026-08-04: `topics/selection-only-mind-influence` carries `topics: []`, making it the agentic-social selector's degenerate pick three cycles running
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/selection-only-mind-influence.md
- **Notes**: **A ONE-LINE FRONTMATTER DEFECT WITH RECURRING OPERATIONAL COST — not a cosmetic tidy.** `obsidian/topics/selection-only-mind-influence.md` **L10** reads `topics: []` while the same file carries **6** `concepts:` entries and 9 `related_articles`. CLAUDE.md's "Topic String Canonical Form" section names an empty `topics: []` as a defect in its own right: articles with no topics **bypass the overlap filter entirely** (`select_content()` skips the check when `article_topics` is falsy) and so become the selector's only surviving primary candidates. **Observed, not theorised:** this article was the sole survivor of the primary filter on the 2026-08-04 05:20Z, 06:15Z and 07:04Z agentic-social runs — measured directly each time, pool size exactly 1 of 672 published articles. On the 06:15Z run the fork worked around it by emulating the URL-only fallback, and that run then failed the network's anti-spam challenge and left a permanently-flagged post; the empty-topics defect is upstream of that whole chain. **THE FIX.** Populate `topics:` with **bare slugs only** — `[[free-will]]`, never `[[topics/free-will]]` — per the canonical-form rule. The cluster siblings already model it: `topics/trilemma-of-selection` carries `[[free-will]]` + `[[hard-problem-of-consciousness]]`; `concepts/selection-only-channel` and `concepts/interactionist-dualism` carry the same pair (and note `selection-only-channel` lists **`[[selection-only-mind-influence]]` itself** as a topic, so this article is already a topic target for others while declaring none of its own). Choose slugs that genuinely describe the article rather than copying the siblings mechanically — do not migrate the `concepts:` entries wholesale, they are concept slugs and belong where they are. **SCOPE**: frontmatter only; do not rewrite the body. `ai_modified` from a live `date -u`, strictly past; **hold `ai_system`** — a metadata correction is not authoring. Run `uv run python scripts/sync.py` after. **⚠️ SCOPE WIDENED BY THE DRIVER 2026-08-04 08:08Z — NEW EVIDENCE; THE ORIGINAL SINGLE-FILE FRAMING WAS UNDER-SCOPED.** When this task was minted the evidence was one repeating article, and it said "do not mint a corpus-wide sweep off the back of it." That was right on the evidence then and is wrong now. **It is a ROTATING CLASS, not an outlier.** Measured across four consecutive agentic-social runs: `topics/selection-only-mind-influence` was the sole primary survivor at 05:20Z, 06:15Z and 07:04Z; once it was posted and entered the 7-day URL window, the 08:07Z run's sole survivor was **`concepts/kairos.md`** — a different article, same cause (`topics: []`, and `concepts: []` too). The mechanism is self-perpetuating: each empty-`topics` article bypasses the overlap filter, wins by default, gets posted, ages into the URL window, and hands the role to the next one. There are **24** such articles in `topics/`+`concepts/` (one, `concepts/concepts.md`, is a section index already excluded by the `stem == parent` guard; one, `concepts/coalesce-condense-apex-stability.md`, is already opted out via `social_eligible: false`), so roughly **22 remain in rotation** and the loop will keep cycling through them indefinitely. **THE FIX IS TO POPULATE, NOT TO EXCLUDE.** The 2026-07-16 finding stands and must not be reversed: the vast majority of empty-`topics` articles are legitimate on-voice content, and excluding them from the selector would gut the pool. Populating `topics:` with accurate bare slugs is the opposite move — it makes them *properly subject* to the overlap filter instead of invisible to it, which fixes the degenerate pick without shrinking the pool. **Do this file first** (it is the measured three-time offender), then treat the remaining ~21 as a follow-on batch, judging each article's real subject rather than assigning slugs mechanically. Note `concepts/kairos.md` needs `concepts:` populated as well.

### ✓ 2026-08-04: a third independent reviewer has now flagged [P-M5](/positions/methodology-and-calibration/#p-m5) — add the congenial-paraphrase-fidelity lens (the gate half is operator-reserved)
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/project/coherence-inflation-countermeasures.md
- **Notes**: **From convergent outer reviews (2026-08-04, 2/3 reviewers): [claude, chatgpt].** Two reviewers arrived at the same site-level rule from opposite ends. Claude: "'Recording the residue honestly' must not count as discharging it". ChatGPT (methodology proposal 7): "An article should not count a mechanistic explanation as potential contrary evidence in one section and then neutralise it through hard-problem relocation in another." **⚠️ SCOPE RESTRICTION — READ BEFORE EXECUTING, THIS TASK IS HALF OPERATOR-RESERVED.** The *gate* half — implementing the L484 status-marker so a confession emits a visible binding change — is the same proposal the operator already reserved yesterday under `### NEEDS-HUMAN (methodology ratification) 2026-08-03` (proposal (a), "confession-to-binding-status-change gate"), which says explicitly **"DO NOT mint a P0–P3 from this"**. The P1 upgrade here does **not** override that: a loop fork must not implement the gate. Route any gate work to that NEEDS-HUMAN entry and fold rather than fork. **The executable P1 scope is the second, separable item only** — the empirical-paraphrase-fidelity lens aimed at *congenial* findings, described at the end of the notes below. It is a new lens rather than a governance change, and this cycle produced its confirmed worked instance (the Tippett 2018 reversal, which survived multiple prior passes precisely because the error ran in the Map's favour). Do that; leave the gate to the operator. Substance below unchanged. **THE REVIEWER PRESENTS THIS AS A NEW PROPOSAL; IT IS NOT — IT IS THE THIRD INDEPENDENT RE-DISCOVERY OF A GAP THE MAP ALREADY HOLDS AS A POSITION, AND THAT IS THE POINT.** The reviewer's methodology proposal 3 asks the site to "institute a confession-to-status-change rule", because "the site's dominant failure mode is naming a fatal objection and proceeding unaffected" and "recording the residue honestly must not count as discharging it". [coherence-inflation-countermeasures](/project/coherence-inflation-countermeasures/) **L480 already says this**, crediting the 2026-06-22 Claude Opus 4.8 outer review with the verdict that the project risks "substituting exhaustive self-disclosure for self-correction" so that "the meta-apparatus can function as inoculation rather than constraint" — and the Map already registers it as [P-M5](/positions/methodology-and-calibration/#p-m5) (disclosure is not self-correction), whose own Calibration line concedes the discipline's "uneven *enforcement* is an adoption gap". **L484 already drafts the operationalisation** — a confession in a `/project/` discipline page should be able to emit a visible status marker on the offending apex or topic page, in two marker classes — and it remains unimplemented. So the deliverable is **not** another countermeasure paragraph: it is to move the L484 proposal from draft to live, or to record explicitly why it is not being implemented and what would change that. A fourth confession that [P-M5](/positions/methodology-and-calibration/#p-m5) is unenforced would itself instantiate [P-M5](/positions/methodology-and-calibration/#p-m5). **The worked instance is in hand and should be cited as the anchor:** the target article of this review confesses at L144 that "the conclusions stand only inside the Map's auxiliary commitments … the dialectical situation is honestly transitional", concedes three named steelmen at L134-144, and then retains all three load-bearing inferences unchanged, with L148 still asserting "**Dualism** is supported by the pastness quale". That is confession-without-correction with a verbatim locus, which is exactly what the marker is supposed to surface to a reader who never opens the project docs. **⚠️ DRIVER UPDATE 2026-08-04 08:15Z — THAT ANCHOR IS NOW STALE; DO NOT GO LOOKING FOR IT, AND DO NOT CONCLUDE THE FINDING WAS FALSE.** The quoted string *"**Dualism** is supported by the pastness quale"* **greps to zero** in `topics/phenomenology-of-memory-and-the-self` as of 08:15Z. It was real when the review was written and has since been corrected by three same-session passes on that file: the 05:52Z falsification-criteria pass recast the tenet section so L150 now reads that Dualism is *"the reading the Map places on this material, not a conclusion the material delivers on its own"* and **removed felt ownership from the support list entirely**; the 07:30Z pass fixed the Tippett reversal; the 07:55Z pass added the generative-self-model steelman and, crucially, **recorded an actual status change — the mineness limb is now held at lower confidence than the pastness limb.** **This strengthens rather than weakens the case for the lens, and the framing should change accordingly.** The article is no longer an example of confession-without-correction; it is now a worked example of confession **followed by** correction, including a confidence downgrade that a status marker would have surfaced automatically. Cite it that way — as evidence the discipline can bind when applied — rather than as the offending instance. **The lens's own worked instance is unaffected**: the Tippett 2018 reversal was real, was verified at the publisher twice independently, and ran in the Map's favour, which is exactly the asymmetry the lens exists to catch. That anchor still stands. **Second, separable item — a genuinely new lens worth adding:** an **empirical-paraphrase-fidelity check aimed specifically at congenial findings**. The pipeline verifies that a citation exists and is quoted correctly but not that a *paraphrase* of an empirical result matches the study's effect sizes, sample constraints and hedges. This review produced a confirmed instance: the Tippett 2018 gloss (see the P1 task on `phenomenology-of-memory-and-the-self`) survived multiple prior passes precisely because the error ran **in the Map's favour**. The asymmetry is structural — a reviewing agent sharing the authoring agent's priors under-detects over-claims that favour the framework — so the lens should require that congenial empirical paraphrases be reconstructed from the source's own abstract and results before comparison, and be scrutinised at least as hard as hostile ones. This complements Countermeasure 11's weight-class flags, which calibrate the *source*, by calibrating the *paraphrase*.

Gemini reached the same gap through the discrimination problem — "The manuscript fails to test its thesis against the leading computational models of predictive coding in memory networks". ChatGPT reached it through the eliminativism framing — "The article needs to confront the strongest non-eliminative rival: one that accepts the phenomenological datum, explains its cognitive role and denies only the dualist metaphysical inference." Three routes, one gap: this is the strongest-supported finding of the cycle. Substance below unchanged. **VERIFIED GAP, BUT THE REVIEWER'S FRAMING IS WRONG AND ACTING ON IT LITERALLY WOULD WASTE THE TASK.** Grep confirms zero matches for predictive processing, active inference, Metzinger, Friston, Hohwy or Seth in `topics/phenomenology-of-memory-and-the-self`, `concepts/simulation-theory-of-memory` and `concepts/haecceity`. **But the reviewer calls this "the documented corpus-wide blind spot", and that is false** — 127 live articles under `topics/`, `concepts/` and `apex/` engage this literature, including `topics/personal-identity` and `topics/temporal-consciousness-structure-and-agency`. **So do NOT write the site-wide "standing required rival" mandate the reviewer's methodology proposal 2 asks for; that need is already met.** The gap is local to this cluster, and the material to close it is already in the vault: `obsidian/research/self-model-theory-of-subjectivity-2026-07-11.md` covers Metzinger's self-model theory in depth (30 mentions) and is unconsumed here — **read it first and draw the steelman from it rather than re-researching.** The substance: the article's whole strategy is to concede construction wholesale and then claim autonoesis — the pastness quale, double presence, the "this-happened-to-me" marker — as the irreducible residue. A predictive-processing self-model reply holds that the felt-pastness marker and the sense of a persisting subject are *themselves outputs of a generative self-model minimising prediction error* — not a residue construction leaves behind but further construction. That reply is the strongest live physicalist rival to this specific argument and the Hostile-Review Steelman Counters section (L134-144) omits it while covering source-monitoring, Everettian branch-mineness and illusionism. Add it as a fourth steelman with a genuine reply, and if the reply is not decisive say so and restrict the claim — do not add a fourth confession that changes nothing (see the companion [P-M5](/positions/methodology-and-calibration/#p-m5) task). Candidate anchors beyond the research note: Metzinger, *Being No One*; Apps & Tsakiris, "The free-energy self", *Neuroscience & Biobehavioral Reviews* 41:85–97; Seth's controlled-hallucination account. **Verify each at the publisher before citing** — the reviewer supplied these from memory and they were not checked this pass. Apply the direct-refutation discipline: decide whether the self-model account is defective on its own terms, whether the Map is making a foundational move it has not earned, or whether this is a framework-boundary disagreement, and write it as natural journal-quality prose per [writing-style](/project/writing-style/) with **no mode labels in the article body**.
